All seven bottom-sheet-hosting fragments** now implement the interface and delegate to the helper: `InvertPdfFragment`, `RemovePagesFragment`, `RemoveDuplicatePagesFragment`, `SplitFilesFragment`, `AddImagesFragment`, `MergeFilesFragment`, and `PdfToImageFragment` (which uses the differently named `mSheetBehavior` field).\n\n## Resulting behaviour\n\n- Sheet open → back collapses the sheet, staying on the same fragment (expected behaviour from the report).\n- Sheet already collapsed → back returns to `HomeFragment`, as before.\n- On `HomeFragment` → double-back-to-exit still works; fragments without a sheet are unaffected since the activity only calls into fragments implementing the interface.\n\n## Verification\n\n- `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL** (needed JDK 8; the wrapper\'s Gradle version rejects the default JDK 17 — unrelated to this change).\n- `./gradlew checkstyle --rerun-tasks` → **0 violations**, so the project\'s style gate (`check`) stays green.', structured_output=None, model_usage={'claude-opus-5': {'inputTokens': 64, 'outputTokens': 17038, 'cacheReadInputTokens': 932403, 'cacheCreationInputTokens': 44687, 'webSearchRequests': 0, 'costUSD': 1.17176525, 'contextWindow': 1000000, 'maxOutputTokens': 64000, 'thinkingTokens': 2485, 'canonicalModel': 'claude-opus-5', 'provider': 'firstParty', 'costBasis': 'list'}}, permission_denials=[], deferred_tool_use=None, errors=None, api_error_status=None, uuid='51d756c1-9a60-4f59-b4be-b873e79833a5')
